

SQL> --查询表的大小
SQL> col OWNER for a20
SQL> col SEGMENT_NAME for a30
SQL> col SEGMENT_TYPE for a30
SQL> col tablespace_name for a20
SQL> set lines 200 pages 999 long 20000
SQL> select  OWNER,SEGMENT_NAME,SEGMENT_TYPE,tablespace_name ,sum(BYTES)/1024/1024/1024 
from dba_segments
where segment_name='ARC_ES_CONSPRC_TACT_SNAP' and owner='FDPSHARE'
group by OWNER,SEGMENT_NAME,SEGMENT_TYPE,tablespace_name order by sum(BYTES);

OWNER                SEGMENT_NAME                   SEGMENT_TYPE                   TABLESPACE_NAME      SUM(BYTES)/1024/1024/1024
-------------------- ------------------------------ ------------------------------ -------------------- -------------------------
FDPSHARE             ARC_ES_CONSPRC_TACT_SNAP       TABLE SUBPARTITION             SHAREDATA                           469.447266

SQL> --查询索引大小(已经在索引表空间了,不需要移动表空间)
SQL> col INDEX_NAME for a30
SQL> col DEGREE for a5
SQL> col tablespace_name for a30
SQL> col status for a10
SQL> set lines 200 pages 999 long 20000
SQL> select index_name,degree,status,tablespace_name 
from dba_indexes 
where owner='FDPSHARE' and table_name='ARC_ES_CONSPRC_TACT_SNAP';

IND_ARC_ECT_SNAP_YM0708        1     N/A

SQL> --有子分区索引,查看子分区索引信息
SQL> col index_name for a30
SQL> col tablespace_name for a30
SQL> col status for a10
SQL> select distinct index_name,status,tablespace_name from dba_ind_subpartitions where index_owner='FDPSHARE' 
and  index_name in ('IND_ARC_ECT_SNAP_CI0708','IND_ARC_ECT_SNAP_YM0708');

INDEX_NAME                     STATUS     TABLESPACE_NAME

SQL> col OWNER for a20
SQL> col SEGMENT_NAME for a30
SQL> col SEGMENT_TYPE for a20
SQL> set lines 200 pages 999 long 20000

from dba_segments

group by OWNER,SEGMENT_NAME,SEGMENT_TYPE order by sum(BYTES);

SQL> --转移分区表并压缩表的语句
SQL> select 'alter table '||table_owner||'.'||table_name||' move subpartition '||subpartition_name||' 
tablespace HISCMPRS01 nologging parallel 8 compress;',compression,subpartition_name 
from dba_tab_subpartitions 
where table_owner='FDPSHARE' and table_name='ARC_ES_CONSPRC_TACT_SNAP';

from dba_ind_subpartitions t 
where INDEX_OWNER='FDPSHARE' and index_name in ('IND_ARC_ECT_SNAP_CI0708','IND_ARC_ECT_SNAP_YM0708')
